Because the kind precedes the value, ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"uir.Unmarshal"}]," (and every ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"UnmarshalJSON"}],", which delegates to it) decodes in one pass over the document without buffering a subtree to find its discriminator. A slot is a single key; an unknown kind, or a slot holding two kinds, is refused. A slot whose first key does not start with ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"$$"}]," is the legacy inline layout — the value itself, stamped with its discriminator, as the Python and Java producers and documents written before the tagged layout carry it — and still decodes. ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"UIR.ToMap"}]," / ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"AssertionEnv"}]," (and ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"uir.UnwrapSlots"}],") present every slot inline, so assertions see one shape whichever layout a document uses."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":[["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"node_kind"}]," is the node discriminator. It is the kind the node's concrete Go type is registered under in ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"NodeMarshaler"}],": the node's static ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"GetType()"}]," (",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"package"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"class"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"method"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"record_field"}],", …) for every registered node, and ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"ref"}]," for a ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"NodeRef"}],". It names a ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"Node"}]," slot (",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"{\"$ref\": …}"}],") and is stamped inside the slot's value too. A node nested through a concrete field (",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"UIR.packages"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"TypedNode.methods"}],", …) carries no ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"node_kind"}],", because its Go type is already known. Decoding a ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"Node"}]," slot without a kind, or with one nothing is registered under, is an error rather than a guess."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":[["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"node_type"}]," is ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"Identifier"}]," data, not a discriminator. A ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"MethodNode"}]," may carry ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"node_type: \"constructor\""}],", and a ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"NodeRef"}]," carries the ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"node_type"}]," of the node it points at, so ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"{\"node_kind\": \"ref\", \"node_type\": \"method\", \"method\": \"GetUser\"}"}]," decodes as a reference to a method, never as a ","$L138",". 135:["$","p",null,{"data-v":"","children":"When implementing a new extractor or generator, make sure the emitted UIR answers these questions:"}]
136:["$","ul",null,{"data-v":"","children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":"What is the root ownership structure: module, package, type, or root-level node?"}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":"Does every emitted declaration have the right identifier components?"}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":"Does every declaration carry a path and location when the source is known?"}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":["Are function signatures modeled with ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"params"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"returns"}],", and ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"returnType"}]," instead of freeform text?"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":["Are external integrations represented as ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"ASTEndpoint"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"EndpointCallStmt"}],"?"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":["Are data schemas represented as ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"ASTRecord"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"RecordField"}],"?"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":"Are cross-node links represented by refs or relationships instead of duplicated nodes?"}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":["Are language-specific leftovers isolated in ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"rawType"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"properties"}],", or ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"RawStmt"}]," instead of leaking into core fields?"]}],"\n"]}]

13b:["$","li",null,{"data-v":"","children":["A statement whose ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"Type"}]," is refined past its registered kind keeps ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"statement_type"}]," at the kind and carries the refined value in ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"statement_refinement"}],", e.g. ",["$","code",null,{"data-v":true,"children":"{\"statement_type\": \"call\", \"statement_refinement\": \"call:package\"}"}],".
